# #1180 lexical package import-prefix checkpoint

The frozen [#1128 import-prefix profile](typer-classpath-import-prefix-profile-3.9.0.md) remains unchanged. This post-#1168–#1175 checkpoint starts from its exact weighted source rows and blocker origins, then collects current resolver outcomes; it does not treat a moved first blocker as full-method success.

Both normalized profiles, reconciliation, and generated sidecars matched byte-for-byte. Inputs are pinned to Scala revision `777528f19a58e794c9954a42f433373472ec57f8`, Scala artifacts 3.9.0, and JDK 21.0.8. The full-audit typed-local-method denominator remains 3,297.

## Frozen cohort reconciliation

| Measure | Frozen #1128 | Current |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Weighted first-segment observations | 2394 | 1063 unresolved next-segment observations |
| Files | 209 | 81 |
| Target keys | 30 | 31 |
| Distinct blocker origins | 904 | 393 |

The exact frozen-row reconciliation contains `2394` rows: `721` still fail with `ImportQualifierNotFound` at the same import tree, and `1673` have advanced to a later current method blocker. Of those advanced rows, `58` include a matching resolver request and selected package identity for the frozen qualifier. Selector outcomes are tracked separately: `0` selectors have an observed final selected symbol and `2471` have no exact singular selected symbol recorded (wildcards are not reduced to one symbol). The full-method typed boolean is true for `0` frozen rows. The separate unresolved-only view contains `1063` rows, including `342` newly observed imports outside the frozen cohort. Across the selected roots there are `721` unresolved-prefix observations, `0` term/object materialization gaps, and `0` without a matching failed resolver trace. The full local-method audit still types 0 / 3,297 methods; blocker movement is not full-method success.

## Historical qualified-candidate cohort

The frozen #1128 source-qualified cohort is joined independently from the remaining rows. It retains all 1,615 historically evidenced observations, with current package identity and member selection reported only when the audit observed them. A missing current identity is not treated as successful lexical resolution.

## Current evidence and next slice

The current unresolved-prefix view has `0` source-package registry hits across its 31 target keys. Separately, the frozen historical qualified cohort retains 1,615 rows with direct class/TASTy evidence at source-context candidates. None of those 1,615 rows has a current selected package identity recorded; their movement past the original first-segment failure is not, by itself, causal proof of lexical package resolution.

This checkpoint preserves current-package precedence, `_root_` behavior, ambiguity errors, and rollback evidence. It does not select a next sprint; the remaining DoD points need their own refreshed blocker and loading-performance evidence before that recommendation.


Scala 3.9 oracle fixtures confirm that a package-relative path in the current package wins over a root path, root lookup succeeds when that relative path is absent, stacked package clauses expose their enclosing package scope to an import qualifier, and a single dotted package clause does not synthesize that parent scope. Intermediate packages can exist only through descendants, and a missing selected member remains an error after package resolution. An object/package same-name collision is rejected. The measured cohort has no root-qualified imports, no direct-resource resolver rejection, and no non-package first-segment or ambiguous resolver outcome; `_root_` behavior is covered by the separate oracle fixture. These findings do not promise that all imports or typer failures will be fixed.
